# Questions 2 and 3: scores with 95% intervals and paired tests

Paired cluster bootstrap (10000 draws) and approximate randomization (20000 rounds), seed 0, clusters of the clean test set. Fine-tuned BERT: mean of the three training seeds. Holm's correction within each family: Question 2 216 tests, Question 3 84 tests, Fine-tuned BERT 144 tests (for each of F1, F2 and leak rate).

Established: corrected p below 0.05, interval excluding 0, and a difference of at least 0.01.
